Extract digits only: \"32명\" -> \"32\"\n        digits = re.search(r'\\d+', value)\n        replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = digits.group() if digits else value\n    elif key == '만족도':\n        # Extract numeric score: \"4.5/5.0\" -> \"4.5점 (5.0점 만점)\"\n        match = re.search(r'([\\d.]+)', value)\n        if match:\n            score = match.group(1)\n            replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = f'{score}점 (5.0점 만점)'\n        else:\n            replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = value\n    elif key == '종합의견':\n        # Append \" 후속 심화반 검토 요망.\" with proper spacing\n        if value.endswith(' '):\n            replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = value + '후속 심화반 검토 요망.'\n        else:\n            replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = value + ' 후속 심화반 검토 요망.'\n    else:\n        replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = value\n\nprint(\"Replacement map:\")\nfor k, v in replacements.items():\n    print(f\"  {k} -> {v}\")\n\n# Process each section XML\nsection_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'Contents')) if f.startswith('section') and f.endswith('.xml')])\n\nfor section_file in section_files:\n    filepath = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'Contents', section_file)\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {section_file}...\")\n\n    # Parse XML\n    tree = ET.parse(filepath)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified_paragraphs = set()\n\n    # Walk all elements and replace placeholders in text/tail\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        # Check and replace in .text\n        if elem.text and '{{' in elem.text:\n            for placeholder, replacement in replacements.items():\n                if placeholder in elem.text:\n                    elem.text = elem.text.replace(placeholder, replacement)\n                    # Find parent paragraph\n                    parent = root.find(f\".//*[@id='{elem.get('id')}']..\") if elem.get('id') else None\n                    # Actually, traverse up to find hp:p\n                    for p in root.iter(f'{{{HWP_NS}}}p'):\n                        for child in p.iter():\n                            if child is elem:\n                                modified_paragraphs.add(p)\n                                break\n\n        # Check and replace in .tail\n        if elem.tail and '{{' in elem.tail:\n            for placeholder, replacement in replacements.items():\n                if placeholder in elem.tail:\n                    elem.tail = elem.tail.replace(placeholder, replacement)\n\n    # Re-walk to find all paragraphs containing replaced text\n    # (simpler approach: check all paragraphs for remaining '{{')\n    for p in root.iter(f'{{{HWP_NS}}}p'):\n        # Convert paragraph to string to check for placeholders\n        text_content = ''.join([t.text or '' for t in p.iter()])\n        if any(ph in text_content for ph in replacements.keys()):\n            modified_paragraphs.add(p)\n\n    # Remove lineSegArray from modified paragraphs\n    for p in modified_paragraphs:\n        for linesegarray in p.findall(f'{{{HWP_NS}}}linesegarray'):\n            p.remove(linesegarray)\n\n    # Write back XML with proper declaration\n    tree.write(filepath, xml_declaration=True, encoding='UTF-8')\n    print(f\"  Modified {len(modified_paragraphs)} paragraphs, removed lineSegArray elements\")\n\n# Verify no placeholders remain\nprint(\"\\nVerifying no placeholders remain...\")\nplaceholder_count = 0\nfor section_file in section_files:\n    filepath = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'Contents', section_file)\n    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n        if '{{' in content:\n            placeholder_count += content.count('{{')\n            print(f\"  WARNING: Found {{...}} in {section_file}\")\n\nif placeholder_count == 0:\n    print(\"  OK: No placeholders remain\")\nelse:\n    print(f\"  ERROR: {placeholder_count} placeholders remain\")\n\n# Repackage HWPX\nprint(f\"\\nRepackaging to {OUTPUT_PATH}...\")\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, 'w') as zf:\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1783086062-J6he8oL7FURt2t6Qb0vY","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"Write:633b479c","caller":{"type":"direct"},"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/hwpx_work/fill_template.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Fill HWPX template with JSON data.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport xml.etree.ElementTree as ET\nimport zipfile\n\nBASE_DIR = '/tmp/hwpx_work/template_contents'\nOUTPUT_PATH = '/root/training_feedback_ready.hwpx'\n\n# Register namespace BEFORE any parsing to prevent tag renaming\nHWP_NS = 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'\nET.register_namespace('hp', HWP_NS)\n\n# Load JSON data\nwith open('/root/training_feedback.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# Build replacement map\nreplacements = {}\n\nfor key, value in data.items():\n    if key == '참석자수':\n        # Extract digits only: \"32명\" -> \"32\"\n        digits = re.search(r'\\d+', value)\n        replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = digits.group() if digits else value\n    elif key == '만족도':\n        # Extract numeric score: \"4.5/5.0\" -> \"4.5점 (5.0점 만점)\"\n        match = re.search(r'([\\d.]+)', value)\n        if match:\n            score = match.group(1)\n            replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = f'{score}점 (5.0점 만점)'\n        else:\n            replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = value\n    elif key == '종합의견':\n        # Append \" 후속 심화반 검토 요망.\" with proper spacing\n        if value.endswith(' '):\n            replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = value + '후속 심화반 검토 요망.'\n        else:\n            replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = value + ' 후속 심화반 검토 요망.'\n    else:\n        replacements[f'{{{key}}}'] = value\n\nprint(\"Replacement map:\")\nfor k, v in replacements.items():\n    print(f\"  {k} -> {v}\")\n\n# Process each section XML\nsection_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'Contents')) if f.startswith('section') and f.endswith('.xml')])\n\nfor section_file in section_files:\n    filepath = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'Contents', section_file)\n    print(f\"\\nProcessing {section_file}...\")\n\n    # Parse XML\n    tree = ET.parse(filepath)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified_paragraphs = set()\n    hp_p = f'{{{HWP_NS}}}p'\n    hp_linesegarray = f'{{{HWP_NS}}}linesegarray'\n\n    # Walk all elements and find those containing placeholders\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        has_placeholder = False\n\n        # Check and replace in .text\n        if elem.text:\n            new_text = elem.text\n            for placeholder, replacement in replacements.items():\n                if placeholder in new_text:\n                    new_text = new_text.replace(placeholder, replacement)\n                    has_placeholder = True\n            if has_placeholder:\n                elem.text = new_text\n\n        # Check and replace in .tail\n        if elem.tail:\n            new_tail = elem.tail\n            for placeholder, replacement in replacements.items():\n                if placeholder in new_tail:\n                    new_tail = new_tail.replace(placeholder, replacement)\n                    has_placeholder = True\n            if has_placeholder:\n                elem.tail = new_tail\n\n        # If this element had a placeholder, find its parent paragraph\n        if has_placeholder:\n            # Find parent paragraph by traversing up\n            def find_parent(element, parent_map):\n                for parent in root.iter():\n                    for child in parent:\n                        if child is element:\n                            return parent\n                return None\n\n            # Build parent map\n            parent_map = {c: p for p in root.iter() for c in p}\n            current = elem\n            while current is not None:\n                if current.tag == hp_p:\n                    modified_paragraphs.add(id(current))\n                    break\n                current = parent_map.get(current)\n\n    #
